  THE PIN — every claim below was asserted before a game ran, PER ARM:
    middle   REAL dice, seeded and shared by CATEGORY (acc / crit / sec / dmg / stall) so both engines draw the same values for the same kind of roll. Moves miss at their printed accuracy, secondaries fire at their printed chance, paralysis does NOT end a body, and games END. A game whose draw counts diverge is VOID, not a divergence.
      ok  SAME CONTEXT, SAME VALUE -- the claim every other one rests on
      ok  a different context gives a different value
      ok  the nth index separates repeats -- 6 of 20 authority draws share a context without it
      ok  every value lands inside [0,1)
      ok  the hash is UNIFORM enough to price a 90-accuracy move  [2,000 contexts, +/- 5 points]
      ok  and to price a 30% secondary  [2,000 contexts, +/- 5 points]
      ok  a certainty is still a certainty: 100 accuracy always hits, 0 never does
      ok  the category wrapper ATTACHED — an unattached one silently buckets every roll as ANY
      ok  ...and the installed wrapper writes into THIS module load’s holder
      ok  the RANGE form consumes NO shared address in this arm  [MEDI_MID_RANGE_DRAWS=1 restores it]
      ok  the SELF-DROP roll has its OWN address bucket  [MEDI_MID_SELFDROP_SHARED=1 restores it]
      ok  the RANGE form is pinned under `sec` too, not only `any`  [a confusion from a SECONDARY]
      ok  `BattleActions#getConfusionDamage` is wrapped as `dmg`  [MEDI_CONFUSION_DMG_CAT_LEGACY=1 removes it]
      ok  and a `random(16)` taken under `dmg` comes back INVERTED — the sixteen-index convention both engines share, not `floor(u*16)`

    the target is 100%. Denominator is EVERY game: 0 never reached a turn-1 boundary (the board parted at the leads, or the battle ended) and
    those count against. Of the 961 that did reach one, 955 agreed — 99.4%.

    THE DECAY, TURN BY TURN. The denominator is stated at each entry: games that REACHED
    that turn. Under the stop rule, reaching turn N means the board was identical through
    N-1, so the right-hand column is "given we still agree entering this turn, do we
    still agree after it". A game that ended early counts as agreement nowhere.

  (Will: "how much is just medicham being semantic"). The game is played to the turn cap
  or to the end of the battle WHATEVER either comparator already found, and the LAST
  board both engines produced is compared. It is a claim about where they ARRIVED and
  not that every line after the mismatch agreed.

  ROADMAP #80 — THE KNOCK OFF ORDERING, ASKED AS TWO INDEPENDENT HALVES:
    boost      x1.5 for holding an item        showdown 1.495   medicham 1.495   (expected 1.5)
    reduction  x0.5 Colbur vs super-eff Dark   showdown 0.5   medicham 0.5   (expected 0.5)
    net        the two multiplied              showdown 0.747   medicham 0.747   (expected 0.75)
    ASSERTED APART ON PURPOSE: if the 1.5x were also evaluated after removal the two errors
    would partially cancel and the net would look fine. It does not, and they do not.
    THE PREDICTED DAMAGE BUG DOES NOT REPRODUCE. medicham2 prices BOTH halves correctly,
    because playerAction computes the damage RANGE at click time — before the item is
    stripped — so the ordering costs no damage here. What differs is the item's DISPOSITION:
      Leftovers    showdown ["Leftovers|[from] move: Knock Off|[of] p1a: Incineroar"]
                   medicham ["leftovers|[from] move: knockoff|[of] p1a: Incineroar"]
      Colbur Berry showdown ["Colbur Berry|[eat]","Colbur Berry|[weaken]"]
                   medicham ["colburberry|[eat]","colburberry|[weaken]"]
    Showdown records Colbur as EATEN BY ITSELF ([eat] then [weaken]) so Knock Off finds nothing
    left to take; medicham2 records it as KNOCKED OFF. Same end state, different FACT — and
    "was it eaten" is what Harvest, Recycle, Belch, Cud Chew and Unburden read.
    SITRUS, the opposite side of the same line: both engines strip it and NEITHER heals — agrees exactly.

  HOW MUCH OF THE DIVERGENCE IS JUST WORDING — the END STATE, per arm, never pooled
  (Will: "how much is just medicham being semantic"). The game is played to the turn cap
  or to the end of the battle WHATEVER either comparator already found, and the LAST
  board both engines produced is compared. It is a claim about where they ARRIVED and
  not that every line after the mismatch agreed.

      of those 210:
          142  SAME END STATE — the mismatch was WORDING            67.6% of all parted, 69.3% of those with a comparable end
           63  DIFFERENT END STATE — they played a different battle  30.0%
            4  ENDED APART — ONE engine ended the battle and the other did not.
               THIS IS NOT COSMETIC AND IT IS NOT COUNTED EITHER WAY. A game with no
               comparable final board is a THIRD answer, not a rounding decision.
            0  NO COMPARABLE BOARD — no boundary was ever taken
            1  THREW — the harness could not finish the game

      HOW BAD, NOT HOW MANY — the 71 DIFFERENT-END-STATE games, banded
      by what the difference MEANS for the game. ENDED-APART is NOT in here: it has no
      comparable final board and is counted above as a third answer.
        the band-3 threshold is MEASURED, not picked: the median single hit the
        AUTHORITY narrated in this arm is 32.8% of a health bar, over 14,712 hits
        (quartiles 18.2% / 54.1%, p90 83.0%)

  ROADMAP #80 — THE KNOCK OFF ORDERING, ASKED AS TWO INDEPENDENT HALVES:
    boost      x1.5 for holding an item        showdown 1.495   medicham 1.495   (expected 1.5)
    reduction  x0.5 Colbur vs super-eff Dark   showdown 0.5   medicham 0.5   (expected 0.5)
    net        the two multiplied              showdown 0.747   medicham 0.747   (expected 0.75)
    ASSERTED APART ON PURPOSE: if the 1.5x were also evaluated after removal the two errors
    would partially cancel and the net would look fine. It does not, and they do not.
    THE PREDICTED DAMAGE BUG DOES NOT REPRODUCE. medicham2 prices BOTH halves correctly,
    because playerAction computes the damage RANGE at click time — before the item is
    stripped — so the ordering costs no damage here. What differs is the item's DISPOSITION:
      Leftovers    showdown ["Leftovers|[from] move: Knock Off|[of] p1a: Incineroar"]
                   medicham ["leftovers|[from] move: knockoff|[of] p1a: Incineroar"]
      Colbur Berry showdown ["Colbur Berry|[eat]","Colbur Berry|[weaken]"]
                   medicham ["colburberry|[eat]","colburberry|[weaken]"]
    Showdown records Colbur as EATEN BY ITSELF ([eat] then [weaken]) so Knock Off finds nothing
    left to take; medicham2 records it as KNOCKED OFF. Same end state, different FACT — and
    "was it eaten" is what Harvest, Recycle, Belch, Cud Chew and Unburden read.
    SITRUS, the opposite side of the same line: both engines strip it and NEITHER heals — agrees exactly.

    the target is 100%. Denominator is EVERY game: 0 never reached a turn-1 boundary (the board parted at the leads, or the battle ended) and
    those count against. Of the 961 that did reach one, 955 agreed — 99.4%.

    THE DECAY, TURN BY TURN. The denominator is stated at each entry: games that REACHED
    that turn. Under the stop rule, reaching turn N means the board was identical through
    N-1, so the right-hand column is "given we still agree entering this turn, do we
    still agree after it". A game that ended early counts as agreement nowhere.

  (Will: "how much is just medicham being semantic"). The game is played to the turn cap
  or to the end of the battle WHATEVER either comparator already found, and the LAST
  board both engines produced is compared. It is a claim about where they ARRIVED and
  not that every line after the mismatch agreed.

  ROADMAP #80 — THE KNOCK OFF ORDERING, ASKED AS TWO INDEPENDENT HALVES:
    boost      x1.5 for holding an item        showdown 1.495   medicham 1.495   (expected 1.5)
    reduction  x0.5 Colbur vs super-eff Dark   showdown 0.5   medicham 0.5   (expected 0.5)
    net        the two multiplied              showdown 0.747   medicham 0.747   (expected 0.75)
    ASSERTED APART ON PURPOSE: if the 1.5x were also evaluated after removal the two errors
    would partially cancel and the net would look fine. It does not, and they do not.
    THE PREDICTED DAMAGE BUG DOES NOT REPRODUCE. medicham2 prices BOTH halves correctly,
    because playerAction computes the damage RANGE at click time — before the item is
    stripped — so the ordering costs no damage here. What differs is the item's DISPOSITION:
      Leftovers    showdown ["Leftovers|[from] move: Knock Off|[of] p1a: Incineroar"]
                   medicham ["leftovers|[from] move: knockoff|[of] p1a: Incineroar"]
      Colbur Berry showdown ["Colbur Berry|[eat]","Colbur Berry|[weaken]"]
                   medicham ["colburberry|[eat]","colburberry|[weaken]"]
    Showdown records Colbur as EATEN BY ITSELF ([eat] then [weaken]) so Knock Off finds nothing
    left to take; medicham2 records it as KNOCKED OFF. Same end state, different FACT — and
    "was it eaten" is what Harvest, Recycle, Belch, Cud Chew and Unburden read.
    SITRUS, the opposite side of the same line: both engines strip it and NEITHER heals — agrees exactly.

  HOW MUCH OF THE DIVERGENCE IS JUST WORDING — the END STATE, per arm, never pooled
  (Will: "how much is just medicham being semantic"). The game is played to the turn cap
  or to the end of the battle WHATEVER either comparator already found, and the LAST
  board both engines produced is compared. It is a claim about where they ARRIVED and
  not that every line after the mismatch agreed.

    ARM middle   961 games, 210 whose protocol parted
      of those 210:
          142  SAME END STATE — the mismatch was WORDING            67.6% of all parted, 69.3% of those with a comparable end
           63  DIFFERENT END STATE — they played a different battle  30.0%
            4  ENDED APART — ONE engine ended the battle and the other did not.
               THIS IS NOT COSMETIC AND IT IS NOT COUNTED EITHER WAY. A game with no
               comparable final board is a THIRD answer, not a rounding decision.
            0  NO COMPARABLE BOARD — no boundary was ever taken
            1  THREW — the harness could not finish the game

      HOW BAD, NOT HOW MANY — the 71 DIFFERENT-END-STATE games, banded
      by what the difference MEANS for the game. ENDED-APART is NOT in here: it has no
      comparable final board and is counted above as a third answer.
        the band-3 threshold is MEASURED, not picked: the median single hit the
        AUTHORITY narrated in this arm is 32.8% of a health bar, over 14,712 hits

  ROADMAP #80 — THE KNOCK OFF ORDERING, ASKED AS TWO INDEPENDENT HALVES:
    boost      x1.5 for holding an item        showdown 1.495   medicham 1.495   (expected 1.5)
    reduction  x0.5 Colbur vs super-eff Dark   showdown 0.5   medicham 0.5   (expected 0.5)
    net        the two multiplied              showdown 0.747   medicham 0.747   (expected 0.75)
    ASSERTED APART ON PURPOSE: if the 1.5x were also evaluated after removal the two errors
    would partially cancel and the net would look fine. It does not, and they do not.
    THE PREDICTED DAMAGE BUG DOES NOT REPRODUCE. medicham2 prices BOTH halves correctly,
    because playerAction computes the damage RANGE at click time — before the item is
    stripped — so the ordering costs no damage here. What differs is the item's DISPOSITION:
      Leftovers    showdown ["Leftovers|[from] move: Knock Off|[of] p1a: Incineroar"]
                   medicham ["leftovers|[from] move: knockoff|[of] p1a: Incineroar"]
      Colbur Berry showdown ["Colbur Berry|[eat]","Colbur Berry|[weaken]"]
                   medicham ["colburberry|[eat]","colburberry|[weaken]"]
    Showdown records Colbur as EATEN BY ITSELF ([eat] then [weaken]) so Knock Off finds nothing
    left to take; medicham2 records it as KNOCKED OFF. Same end state, different FACT — and
    "was it eaten" is what Harvest, Recycle, Belch, Cud Chew and Unburden read.
    SITRUS, the opposite side of the same line: both engines strip it and NEITHER heals — agrees exactly.

    the target is 100%. Denominator is EVERY game: 0 never reached a turn-1 boundary (the board parted at the leads, or the battle ended) and
    those count against. Of the 961 that did reach one, 955 agreed — 99.4%.

    THE DECAY, TURN BY TURN. The denominator is stated at each entry: games that REACHED
    that turn. Under the stop rule, reaching turn N means the board was identical through
    N-1, so the right-hand column is "given we still agree entering this turn, do we
    still agree after it". A game that ended early counts as agreement nowhere.

  not that every line after the mismatch agreed.

  ROADMAP #80 — THE KNOCK OFF ORDERING, ASKED AS TWO INDEPENDENT HALVES:
    boost      x1.5 for holding an item        showdown 1.495   medicham 1.495   (expected 1.5)
    reduction  x0.5 Colbur vs super-eff Dark   showdown 0.5   medicham 0.5   (expected 0.5)
    net        the two multiplied              showdown 0.747   medicham 0.747   (expected 0.75)
    ASSERTED APART ON PURPOSE: if the 1.5x were also evaluated after removal the two errors
    would partially cancel and the net would look fine. It does not, and they do not.
    THE PREDICTED DAMAGE BUG DOES NOT REPRODUCE. medicham2 prices BOTH halves correctly,
    because playerAction computes the damage RANGE at click time — before the item is
    stripped — so the ordering costs no damage here. What differs is the item's DISPOSITION:
      Leftovers    showdown ["Leftovers|[from] move: Knock Off|[of] p1a: Incineroar"]
                   medicham ["leftovers|[from] move: knockoff|[of] p1a: Incineroar"]
      Colbur Berry showdown ["Colbur Berry|[eat]","Colbur Berry|[weaken]"]
                   medicham ["colburberry|[eat]","colburberry|[weaken]"]
    Showdown records Colbur as EATEN BY ITSELF ([eat] then [weaken]) so Knock Off finds nothing
    left to take; medicham2 records it as KNOCKED OFF. Same end state, different FACT — and
    "was it eaten" is what Harvest, Recycle, Belch, Cud Chew and Unburden read.
    SITRUS, the opposite side of the same line: both engines strip it and NEITHER heals — agrees exactly.

  HOW MUCH OF THE DIVERGENCE IS JUST WORDING — the END STATE, per arm, never pooled
  (Will: "how much is just medicham being semantic"). The game is played to the turn cap
  or to the end of the battle WHATEVER either comparator already found, and the LAST
  board both engines produced is compared. It is a claim about where they ARRIVED and
  not that every line after the mismatch agreed.

      of those 242:
          168  SAME END STATE — the mismatch was WORDING            69.4% of all parted, 71.2% of those with a comparable end
           68  DIFFERENT END STATE — they played a different battle  28.1%
            4  ENDED APART — ONE engine ended the battle and the other did not.
               THIS IS NOT COSMETIC AND IT IS NOT COUNTED EITHER WAY. A game with no
               comparable final board is a THIRD answer, not a rounding decision.

            2  THREW — the harness could not finish the game

  DIRECTED SCENARIOS — the fringe the swarm never reaches (§3.2), and the two findings
  docs/GAME-DIFFERENTIAL-DESIGN.md §5a filed by hand before this driver existed:
    agrees    knock-off order — the item leaves before the HP is subtracted (§5a)
    agrees    contact punish — Rough Skin resolves against the attacker (§5a)
    agrees    resist berry — the berry is spent against the hit it resists (§5a)
    agrees    Electro Shot — the charge is announced before the boost it grants
    agrees    the sandstorm residual is speed-sorted, not slot-ordered
    agrees    Intimidate x guaranteed crit — the crit ignores the attacker's -1 (§6)

  ROADMAP #80 — THE KNOCK OFF ORDERING, ASKED AS TWO INDEPENDENT HALVES:
    boost      x1.5 for holding an item        showdown 1.495   medicham 1.495   (expected 1.5)
    reduction  x0.5 Colbur vs super-eff Dark   showdown 0.5   medicham 0.5   (expected 0.5)
    net        the two multiplied              showdown 0.747   medicham 0.747   (expected 0.75)
    ASSERTED APART ON PURPOSE: if the 1.5x were also evaluated after removal the two errors
    would partially cancel and the net would look fine. It does not, and they do not.
    THE PREDICTED DAMAGE BUG DOES NOT REPRODUCE. medicham2 prices BOTH halves correctly,
    because playerAction computes the damage RANGE at click time — before the item is
    stripped — so the ordering costs no damage here. What differs is the item's DISPOSITION:
      Leftovers    showdown ["Leftovers|[from] move: Knock Off|[of] p1a: Incineroar"]
                   medicham ["leftovers|[from] move: knockoff|[of] p1a: Incineroar"]
      Colbur Berry showdown ["Colbur Berry|[eat]","Colbur Berry|[weaken]"]
                   medicham ["colburberry|[eat]","colburberry|[weaken]"]
    Showdown records Colbur as EATEN BY ITSELF ([eat] then [weaken]) so Knock Off finds nothing
    left to take; medicham2 records it as KNOCKED OFF. Same end state, different FACT — and
    "was it eaten" is what Harvest, Recycle, Belch, Cud Chew and Unburden read.
    SITRUS, the opposite side of the same line: both engines strip it and NEITHER heals — agrees exactly.

  ROADMAP #80 — THE KNOCK OFF ORDERING, ASKED AS TWO INDEPENDENT HALVES:
    boost      x1.5 for holding an item        showdown 1.495   medicham 1.495   (expected 1.5)
    reduction  x0.5 Colbur vs super-eff Dark   showdown 0.5   medicham 0.5   (expected 0.5)
    net        the two multiplied              showdown 0.747   medicham 0.747   (expected 0.75)
    ASSERTED APART ON PURPOSE: if the 1.5x were also evaluated after removal the two errors
    would partially cancel and the net would look fine. It does not, and they do not.
    THE PREDICTED DAMAGE BUG DOES NOT REPRODUCE. medicham2 prices BOTH halves correctly,
    because playerAction computes the damage RANGE at click time — before the item is
    stripped — so the ordering costs no damage here. What differs is the item's DISPOSITION:
      Leftovers    showdown ["Leftovers|[from] move: Knock Off|[of] p1a: Incineroar"]
                   medicham ["leftovers|[from] move: knockoff|[of] p1a: Incineroar"]
      Colbur Berry showdown ["Colbur Berry|[eat]","Colbur Berry|[weaken]"]
                   medicham ["colburberry|[eat]","colburberry|[weaken]"]
    Showdown records Colbur as EATEN BY ITSELF ([eat] then [weaken]) so Knock Off finds nothing
    left to take; medicham2 records it as KNOCKED OFF. Same end state, different FACT — and
    "was it eaten" is what Harvest, Recycle, Belch, Cud Chew and Unburden read.
    SITRUS, the opposite side of the same line: both engines strip it and NEITHER heals — agrees exactly.

  THE TWO DERIVATIONS OF THE TURN-1..3 RATE DISAGREE — one of them is wrong and no number below can be trusted: [{"turn":1,"from_kept_boards":955,"from_the_counters":955,"agree":true},{"turn":2,"from_kept_boards":945,"from_the_counters":945,"agree":true},{"turn":3,"from_kept_boards":928,"from_the_counters":927,"agree":false}]
  ============================================================================
  THE BOARD AT THE END OF TURN 1 IS IDENTICAL IN   955 / 961   99.4%   OF GAMES
  ============================================================================
    the target is 100%. Denominator is EVERY game: 0 never reached a turn-1 boundary (the board parted at the leads, or the battle ended) and
    those count against. Of the 961 that did reach one, 955 agreed — 99.4%.

    THE DECAY, TURN BY TURN. The denominator is stated at each entry: games that REACHED
    that turn. Under the stop rule, reaching turn N means the board was identical through
    N-1, so the right-hand column is "given we still agree entering this turn, do we
    still agree after it". A game that ended early counts as agreement nowhere.
      turn   identical / reached      of all games    given it reached this turn
         1     955 / 961               99.4%           99.4%
         2     945 / 959               98.3%           98.5%
         3     927 / 957               96.5%           96.9%
         4     910 / 951               94.7%           95.7%
         5     872 / 926               90.7%           94.2%
         6     807 / 873               84.0%           92.4%
         7     723 / 788               75.2%           91.8%

  HOW MUCH OF THE DIVERGENCE IS JUST WORDING — the END STATE, per arm, never pooled
  (Will: "how much is just medicham being semantic"). The game is played to the turn cap
  or to the end of the battle WHATEVER either comparator already found, and the LAST
  board both engines produced is compared. It is a claim about where they ARRIVED and
  not that every line after the mismatch agreed.

    ARM middle   961 games, 229 whose protocol parted
      of those 229:
          161  SAME END STATE — the mismatch was WORDING            70.3% of all parted, 71.9% of those with a comparable end
           63  DIFFERENT END STATE — they played a different battle  27.5%
            4  ENDED APART — ONE engine ended the battle and the other did not.
               THIS IS NOT COSMETIC AND IT IS NOT COUNTED EITHER WAY. A game with no
               comparable final board is a THIRD answer, not a rounding decision.
            0  NO COMPARABLE BOARD — no boundary was ever taken
            1  THREW — the harness could not finish the game

  DIRECTED SCENARIOS — the fringe the swarm never reaches (§3.2), and the two findings
  docs/GAME-DIFFERENTIAL-DESIGN.md §5a filed by hand before this driver existed:
    agrees    knock-off order — the item leaves before the HP is subtracted (§5a)
    agrees    contact punish — Rough Skin resolves against the attacker (§5a)
    agrees    resist berry — the berry is spent against the hit it resists (§5a)
    agrees    Electro Shot — the charge is announced before the boost it grants
    agrees    the sandstorm residual is speed-sorted, not slot-ordered
    agrees    Intimidate x guaranteed crit — the crit ignores the attacker's -1 (§6)

  ROADMAP #80 — THE KNOCK OFF ORDERING, ASKED AS TWO INDEPENDENT HALVES:
    boost      x1.5 for holding an item        showdown 1.495   medicham 1.495   (expected 1.5)
    reduction  x0.5 Colbur vs super-eff Dark   showdown 0.5   medicham 0.5   (expected 0.5)
    net        the two multiplied              showdown 0.747   medicham 0.747   (expected 0.75)
    ASSERTED APART ON PURPOSE: if the 1.5x were also evaluated after removal the two errors
    would partially cancel and the net would look fine. It does not, and they do not.
    THE PREDICTED DAMAGE BUG DOES NOT REPRODUCE. medicham2 prices BOTH halves correctly,
    because playerAction computes the damage RANGE at click time — before the item is
    stripped — so the ordering costs no damage here. What differs is the item's DISPOSITION:
      Leftovers    showdown ["Leftovers|[from] move: Knock Off|[of] p1a: Incineroar"]
                   medicham ["leftovers|[from] move: knockoff|[of] p1a: Incineroar"]
      Colbur Berry showdown ["Colbur Berry|[eat]","Colbur Berry|[weaken]"]
                   medicham ["colburberry|[eat]","colburberry|[weaken]"]
    Showdown records Colbur as EATEN BY ITSELF ([eat] then [weaken]) so Knock Off finds nothing
    left to take; medicham2 records it as KNOCKED OFF. Same end state, different FACT — and
    "was it eaten" is what Harvest, Recycle, Belch, Cud Chew and Unburden read.
    SITRUS, the opposite side of the same line: both engines strip it and NEITHER heals — agrees exactly.

  MECHANIC COVERAGE — AND THE NUMBER FELL ON PURPOSE (ROADMAP #91):
    254 / 274  rows whose EFFECT WAS OBSERVED — a board leaf in a family the tag's own params name moved
        across the turn, or a declared negative case was reached and correctly did not fire.
        THIS IS THE COVERAGE NUMBER NOW. It replaces the one below, which counted a CLICK.
    306      rows name NO BOARD LEAF at all (contact, sound, priority, a damage
        multiplier). A connected click is the strongest evidence available for those; 289 got it,
        and they are counted APART and never added into the number above.
    5      rows were CLICKED OR PRESENT AND DID NOTHING — the old counter called every
        one of these covered. Haze into a board with no boosts on it is in here.

  THE OLD COVERAGE NUMBER, KEPT SO THE DROP IS READABLE RATHER THAN A MYSTERY:
    332 / 345  reached by a move that CONNECTED — the engine ran its handler and the stream carries the result
    221 / 235  reached only by an ability or item that was ON THE FIELD. Present is NOT exercised, and the
        median game here parts after 9 completed turn(s), so most of these bodies
        never acted. This half is the weaker claim and is deliberately not added to the first.
    553 / 580 union of the two, stated last because it is the weakest of the three
    63 of the 643 census rows name an INTERACTION rather than a taggable entity and cannot be measured by this
      instrument at all. They are NOT counted as uncovered — a zero on them would read as
      a failure of the run instead of a limit of the measurement.
    clicked and MISSED at least once (the top arm's pin misses every sub-100-accuracy move): 77 moves
    clicked and NEVER ONCE CONNECTED, in any arm — THIS is the unexercised list: 0 moves

  board both engines produced is compared. It is a claim about where they ARRIVED and
  not that every line after the mismatch agreed.

  ROADMAP #80 — THE KNOCK OFF ORDERING, ASKED AS TWO INDEPENDENT HALVES:
    boost      x1.5 for holding an item        showdown 1.495   medicham 1.495   (expected 1.5)
    reduction  x0.5 Colbur vs super-eff Dark   showdown 0.5   medicham 0.5   (expected 0.5)
    net        the two multiplied              showdown 0.747   medicham 0.747   (expected 0.75)
    ASSERTED APART ON PURPOSE: if the 1.5x were also evaluated after removal the two errors
    would partially cancel and the net would look fine. It does not, and they do not.
    THE PREDICTED DAMAGE BUG DOES NOT REPRODUCE. medicham2 prices BOTH halves correctly,
    because playerAction computes the damage RANGE at click time — before the item is
    stripped — so the ordering costs no damage here. What differs is the item's DISPOSITION:
      Leftovers    showdown ["Leftovers|[from] move: Knock Off|[of] p1a: Incineroar"]
                   medicham ["leftovers|[from] move: knockoff|[of] p1a: Incineroar"]
      Colbur Berry showdown ["Colbur Berry|[eat]","Colbur Berry|[weaken]"]
                   medicham ["colburberry|[eat]","colburberry|[weaken]"]
    Showdown records Colbur as EATEN BY ITSELF ([eat] then [weaken]) so Knock Off finds nothing
    left to take; medicham2 records it as KNOCKED OFF. Same end state, different FACT — and
    "was it eaten" is what Harvest, Recycle, Belch, Cud Chew and Unburden read.
    SITRUS, the opposite side of the same line: both engines strip it and NEITHER heals — agrees exactly.

    the target is 100%. Denominator is EVERY game: 0 never reached a turn-1 boundary (the board parted at the leads, or the battle ended) and
    those count against. Of the 961 that did reach one, 955 agreed — 99.4%.

    THE DECAY, TURN BY TURN. The denominator is stated at each entry: games that REACHED
    that turn. Under the stop rule, reaching turn N means the board was identical through
    N-1, so the right-hand column is "given we still agree entering this turn, do we
    still agree after it". A game that ended early counts as agreement nowhere.

  HOW MUCH OF THE DIVERGENCE IS JUST WORDING — the END STATE, per arm, never pooled
  (Will: "how much is just medicham being semantic"). The game is played to the turn cap
  or to the end of the battle WHATEVER either comparator already found, and the LAST
  board both engines produced is compared. It is a claim about where they ARRIVED and
  not that every line after the mismatch agreed.

  DIRECTED SCENARIOS — the fringe the swarm never reaches (§3.2), and the two findings
  docs/GAME-DIFFERENTIAL-DESIGN.md §5a filed by hand before this driver existed:
    agrees    knock-off order — the item leaves before the HP is subtracted (§5a)
    agrees    contact punish — Rough Skin resolves against the attacker (§5a)
    agrees    resist berry — the berry is spent against the hit it resists (§5a)
    agrees    Electro Shot — the charge is announced before the boost it grants
    agrees    the sandstorm residual is speed-sorted, not slot-ordered
    agrees    Intimidate x guaranteed crit — the crit ignores the attacker's -1 (§6)

  ROADMAP #80 — THE KNOCK OFF ORDERING, ASKED AS TWO INDEPENDENT HALVES:
    boost      x1.5 for holding an item        showdown 1.495   medicham 1.495   (expected 1.5)
    reduction  x0.5 Colbur vs super-eff Dark   showdown 0.5   medicham 0.5   (expected 0.5)
    net        the two multiplied              showdown 0.747   medicham 0.747   (expected 0.75)
    ASSERTED APART ON PURPOSE: if the 1.5x were also evaluated after removal the two errors
    would partially cancel and the net would look fine. It does not, and they do not.
    THE PREDICTED DAMAGE BUG DOES NOT REPRODUCE. medicham2 prices BOTH halves correctly,
    because playerAction computes the damage RANGE at click time — before the item is
    stripped — so the ordering costs no damage here. What differs is the item's DISPOSITION:
      Leftovers    showdown ["Leftovers|[from] move: Knock Off|[of] p1a: Incineroar"]
                   medicham ["leftovers|[from] move: knockoff|[of] p1a: Incineroar"]
      Colbur Berry showdown ["Colbur Berry|[eat]","Colbur Berry|[weaken]"]
                   medicham ["colburberry|[eat]","colburberry|[weaken]"]
    Showdown records Colbur as EATEN BY ITSELF ([eat] then [weaken]) so Knock Off finds nothing
    left to take; medicham2 records it as KNOCKED OFF. Same end state, different FACT — and
    "was it eaten" is what Harvest, Recycle, Belch, Cud Chew and Unburden read.
    SITRUS, the opposite side of the same line: both engines strip it and NEITHER heals — agrees exactly.

  THE DAMAGE INTERIOR — measured, not quoted. medicham2 samples its span UNIFORMLY;
  Showdown rolls 16 indices onto the same span and floors each separately:
    knock-off order — the item leaves before the HP is subtracted (§5a)

  MECHANIC COVERAGE — AND THE NUMBER FELL ON PURPOSE (ROADMAP #91):
    254 / 274  rows whose EFFECT WAS OBSERVED — a board leaf in a family the tag's own params name moved
        across the turn, or a declared negative case was reached and correctly did not fire.
        THIS IS THE COVERAGE NUMBER NOW. It replaces the one below, which counted a CLICK.

  HOW MUCH OF THE DIVERGENCE IS JUST WORDING — the END STATE, per arm, never pooled
  (Will: "how much is just medicham being semantic"). The game is played to the turn cap
  or to the end of the battle WHATEVER either comparator already found, and the LAST
  board both engines produced is compared. It is a claim about where they ARRIVED and
  not that every line after the mismatch agreed.

  ROADMAP #80 — THE KNOCK OFF ORDERING, ASKED AS TWO INDEPENDENT HALVES:
    boost      x1.5 for holding an item        showdown 1.495   medicham 1.495   (expected 1.5)
    reduction  x0.5 Colbur vs super-eff Dark   showdown 0.5   medicham 0.5   (expected 0.5)
    net        the two multiplied              showdown 0.747   medicham 0.747   (expected 0.75)
    ASSERTED APART ON PURPOSE: if the 1.5x were also evaluated after removal the two errors
    would partially cancel and the net would look fine. It does not, and they do not.
    THE PREDICTED DAMAGE BUG DOES NOT REPRODUCE. medicham2 prices BOTH halves correctly,
    because playerAction computes the damage RANGE at click time — before the item is
    stripped — so the ordering costs no damage here. What differs is the item's DISPOSITION:
      Leftovers    showdown ["Leftovers|[from] move: Knock Off|[of] p1a: Incineroar"]
                   medicham ["leftovers|[from] move: knockoff|[of] p1a: Incineroar"]
      Colbur Berry showdown ["Colbur Berry|[eat]","Colbur Berry|[weaken]"]
                   medicham ["colburberry|[eat]","colburberry|[weaken]"]
    Showdown records Colbur as EATEN BY ITSELF ([eat] then [weaken]) so Knock Off finds nothing
    left to take; medicham2 records it as KNOCKED OFF. Same end state, different FACT — and
    "was it eaten" is what Harvest, Recycle, Belch, Cud Chew and Unburden read.
    SITRUS, the opposite side of the same line: both engines strip it and NEITHER heals — agrees exactly.
